- BLTP1 (lpd-3) is a BLTP (BicC-like protein) family member that also participates in this complex

The key question is whether this protein has 0, 1, 2, or 3+ transmembrane helices.

Given the overall structure of the protein:
- N-terminal: somewhat mixed hydrophobicity
- Middle: clearly globular (ferredoxin/flavoprotein-like domain)
- C-terminal: clearly polar (with zinc-binding motif)

The only candidate region for a transmembrane helix would be either the N-terminal region or the &quot;IWMNHYLVSFVIVTTATLAYFIFYHIHRL&quot; region in the middle.

The &quot;IWMNHYLVSFVIVTTATLAYFIFYHIHRL&quot; region, while not perfectly hydrophobic, is about 30 residues long and has a moderate hydrophobic character. In some prediction tools, this might be called a weak/short transmembrane helix.
